From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n12\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF32.3, F33.1, F33.3\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, welcome to the clinic. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: I guess I came in because I don’t really feel like myself anymore. I’ve gotten real quiet, don’t talk to people much, and I feel kind of flat most of the time. Even starting my cleaning route at work feels like I’m pushing a heavy cart uphill.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been feeling this way?\n\nPatient: It’s been going on for a while now… about a year and five months, I’d say. It really started building after I had to leave my old apartment and the neighborhood I was used to.\n\nDoctor: Do you still find pleasure in activities that you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I used to look forward to fishing on Saturdays, or just sitting with coffee doing crosswords. Even at work, I’d show the younger guys little tricks for stripping a floor. Now I mostly just let those things sit. They don’t really give me anything back anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience worse mood in the morning?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed it being worse in the morning specifically. It’s more just kind of there most of the time.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel anxious or on edge most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say most of the time. I get uneasy here and there, but I haven’t really kept track of it like that. It’s not the main thing I’ve noticed.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any change in your weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think so. I haven’t been eating much. Most days food just doesn’t taste like anything, so I’ll skip meals or just have some crackers after work. My work pants have been fitting looser than they used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: I don’t have, like, a big plan to do anything. But… a lot of mornings I wake up thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up at all. And that scares me enough that I think I should say it out loud.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving slower or faster than you used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed myself moving faster or anything. Maybe I’m a little slower some days, but I couldn’t say for sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange thoughts or heard voices that others couldn't hear since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have heard things. When I’m mopping after hours, sometimes I’ll hear a man’s voice say my name, or say I missed a spot. It sounds real clear, like he’s standing over by the stairwell. But when I look, nobody’s there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other experiences where you saw, heard, or felt things that weren't really there, besides the voice you mentioned?\n\nPatient: No, not really besides that. It’s mainly the voice at work, after hours—like a man saying my name or telling me I missed a spot. It sounds close, like he’s by the stairwell, but when I check, there’s nobody there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that the voice you hear is somehow controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think the voice is controlling me, exactly. It’s more that I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people in my building are keeping tabs on me, like through the hallway cameras… and maybe passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think people are talking about you or watching you when you're out in public?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly around my building. I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through the hallway cameras, or talking to each other about me somehow. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"

Most days food just doesn’t taste like anything, so I’ll skip meals or just have some crackers after work. My work pants have been fitting looser than they used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: I don’t have, like, a big plan to do anything. But… a lot of mornings I wake up thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up at all. I haven’t noticed myself moving faster or anything. But when I look, nobody’s there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other experiences where you saw, heard, or felt things that weren't really there, besides the voice you mentioned?\n\nPatient: No, not really besides that. It’s mainly the voice at work, after hours—like a man saying my name or telling me I missed a spot. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think people are talking about you or watching you when you're out in public?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly around my building. I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through the hallway cameras, or talking to each other about me somehow. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that things you see or hear on TV, or read in the newspaper, are somehow related to you?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or the newspaper, no. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still feel like it’s about me somehow.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that events or things that happen to other people are somehow related to you or are messages meant for you?\n\nPatient: Not really with other people’s events, no. Now I mostly just let those things sit. They don’t really give me anything back anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience worse mood in the morning?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed it being worse in the morning specifically. It’s more just kind of there most of the time.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel anxious or on edge most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say most of the time. I get uneasy here and there, but I haven’t really kept track of it like that. It’s not the main thing I’ve noticed.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any change in your weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think so. I haven’t been eating much. Most days food just doesn’t taste like anything, so I’ll skip meals or just have some crackers after work. My work pants have been fitting looser than they used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: I don’t have, like, a big plan to do anything. But… a lot of mornings I wake up thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up at all. And that scares me enough that I think I should say it out loud.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving slower or faster than you used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed myself moving faster or anything. Maybe I’m a little slower some days, but I couldn’t say for sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange thoughts or heard voices that others couldn't hear since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have heard things. When I’m mopping after hours, sometimes I’ll hear a man’s voice say my name, or say I missed a spot. It sounds real clear, like he’s standing over by the stairwell. But when I look, nobody’s there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other experiences where you saw, heard, or felt things that weren't really there, besides the voice you mentioned?\n\nPatient: No, not really besides that. It’s mainly the voice at work, after hours—like a man saying my name or telling me I missed a spot. It sounds close, like he’s by the stairwell, but when I check, there’s nobody there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that the voice you hear is somehow controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think the voice is controlling me, exactly. It’s more that I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people in my building are keeping tabs on me, like through the hallway cameras… and maybe passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think people are talking about you or watching you when you're out in public?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly around my building. I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through the hallway cameras, or talking to each other about me somehow. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that things you see or hear on TV, or read in the newspaper, are somehow related to you?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or the newspaper, no. It’s more about my building… I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, or passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still feel like it’s about me somehow.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that events or things that happen to other people are somehow related to you or are messages meant for you?\n\nPatient: Not really with other people’s events, no. It’s more like… in my building, I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through those hallway cameras, or sending messages about me to each other. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that the people you believe are watching you or talking about you are plotting something against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say “plotting,” exactly. It’s more that I feel like the new landlord and maybe a couple people in the building are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, and talking or passing messages about me. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck on it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people you believe are watching you want to harm you?\n\nPatient: I don’t really think they want to hurt me, not exactly. It’s more that I feel like they’re watching me through the hallway cameras and talking about me or passing messages. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck thinking it has to do with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to express your thoughts or get your point across when talking to others?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. Like at work, my supervisor will ask me something simple, like which floors I finished cleaning, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about the old bus route, or the garden plot, or the church basement. And I can hear myself doing it, like I’m not getting back to the actual question. It’s frustrating.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Never bundle several symptoms, or several attributes of one symptom, into a single question — e.g. asking whether a symptom is present and how long it has lasted at the same time.\n- If you need to ask about more than one thing, ask the one that tells you the most now and come back to the rest later.\n- Keep the question clear enough for the patient to understand.\n- Respond in English.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"intent\": \"<what you want to find out with this question>\", \"question\": \"<how you actually ask it in the interview>\"}\n"
  },

    "role": "user",
    "content": "Return the JSON now."
  }

But when I look, nobody’s there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other experiences where you saw, heard, or felt things that weren't really there, besides the voice you mentioned?\n\nPatient: No, not really besides that. It’s mainly the voice at work, after hours—like a man saying my name or telling me I missed a spot. It sounds close, like he’s by the stairwell, but when I check, there’s nobody there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that the voice you hear is somehow controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think the voice is controlling me, exactly. It’s more that I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people in my building are keeping tabs on me, like through the hallway cameras… and maybe passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think people are talking about you or watching you when you're out in public?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly around my building. I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through the hallway cameras, or talking to each other about me somehow. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that things you see or hear on TV, or read in the newspaper, are somehow related to you?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or the newspaper, no. It’s more about my building… I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, or passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still feel like it’s about me somehow.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that events or things that happen to other people are somehow related to you or are messages meant for you?\n\nPatient: Not really with other people’s events, no. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that the people you believe are watching you or talking about you are plotting something against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say “plotting,” exactly. It’s more that I feel like the new landlord and maybe a couple people in the building are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, and talking or passing messages about me. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck on it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people you believe are watching you want to harm you?\n\nPatient: I don’t really think they want to hurt me, not exactly. It’s more that I feel like they’re watching me through the hallway cameras and talking about me or passing messages. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck thinking it has to do with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to express your thoughts or get your point across when talking to others?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. I used to look forward to fishing on Saturdays, or just sitting with coffee doing crosswords. Even at work, I’d show the younger guys little tricks for stripping a floor. Now I mostly just let those things sit. They don’t really give me anything back anymore.\n\nDoctor: Do you experience worse mood in the morning?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed it being worse in the morning specifically. It’s more just kind of there most of the time.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel anxious or on edge most of the time?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say most of the time. I get uneasy here and there, but I haven’t really kept track of it like that. It’s not the main thing I’ve noticed.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any change in your weight since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I think so. I haven’t been eating much. Most days food just doesn’t taste like anything, so I’ll skip meals or just have some crackers after work. My work pants have been fitting looser than they used to.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts about hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: I don’t have, like, a big plan to do anything. But… a lot of mornings I wake up thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up at all. And that scares me enough that I think I should say it out loud.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving slower or faster than you used to?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I haven’t noticed myself moving faster or anything. Maybe I’m a little slower some days, but I couldn’t say for sure.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any strange thoughts or heard voices that others couldn't hear since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I have heard things. When I’m mopping after hours, sometimes I’ll hear a man’s voice say my name, or say I missed a spot. It sounds real clear, like he’s standing over by the stairwell. But when I look, nobody’s there.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any other experiences where you saw, heard, or felt things that weren't really there, besides the voice you mentioned?\n\nPatient: No, not really besides that. It’s mainly the voice at work, after hours—like a man saying my name or telling me I missed a spot. It sounds close, like he’s by the stairwell, but when I check, there’s nobody there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever thought that the voice you hear is somehow controlling your thoughts or actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t think the voice is controlling me, exactly. It’s more that I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people in my building are keeping tabs on me, like through the hallway cameras… and maybe passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but it doesn’t feel that way to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think people are talking about you or watching you when you're out in public?\n\nPatient: Yeah… mostly around my building. I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through the hallway cameras, or talking to each other about me somehow. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that things you see or hear on TV, or read in the newspaper, are somehow related to you?\n\nPatient: Not really TV or the newspaper, no. It’s more about my building… I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, or passing messages about me. People say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still feel like it’s about me somehow.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that events or things that happen to other people are somehow related to you or are messages meant for you?\n\nPatient: Not really with other people’s events, no. It’s more like… in my building, I get stuck thinking the new landlord and a couple people there are watching me through those hallway cameras, or sending messages about me to each other. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but it still feels like they’re keeping tabs on me.\n\nDoctor: Do you think that the people you believe are watching you or talking about you are plotting something against you?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d say “plotting,” exactly. It’s more that I feel like the new landlord and maybe a couple people in the building are keeping tabs on me through the hallway cameras, and talking or passing messages about me. People tell me the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck on it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the people you believe are watching you want to harm you?\n\nPatient: I don’t really think they want to hurt me, not exactly. It’s more that I feel like they’re watching me through the hallway cameras and talking about me or passing messages. I know people say the cameras are just for the front door, but I still get stuck thinking it has to do with me.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to express your thoughts or get your point across when talking to others?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. Like at work, my supervisor will ask me something simple, like which floors I finished cleaning, and I’ll start answering but then I’m talking about the old bus route, or the garden plot, or the church basement. And I can hear myself doing it, like I’m not getting back to the actual question. It’s frustrating.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe the people you think are watching you are actually able to harm you or control your life in any way?\n\nPatient: I don’t think they can control my life, no. Harm me… I don’t really know. It’s more like I get stuck thinking the new landlord and maybe a couple people in the building are watching me through those hallway cameras and passing messages about me. People keep saying the cameras are just for the front door, but I still feel like it’s about me somehow.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about what you mean when you say you start answering a question but then go off on a different topic?\n\nPatient: I mean, like… if my supervisor asks, “Which floors did you finish?” I’ll start to answer, but then my mind goes off. I might start talking about the old bus route, or the garden plot, or something about the church basement, and I’m not really answering what he asked.\n\nI can hear myself doing it sometimes, like I know I’m not getting back to the point, but it’s hard to pull it back. It makes me frustrated because the question was simple.\n\nDoctor: Do you find it hard to stay on topic when talking to others?\n\nPatient: Yes. I do sometimes. Like I said, my supervisor can ask me something simple, like which floors I finished, and I’ll start answering, but then I drift into talking about the old bus route or the garden plot or the church basement. I can hear that I’m not getting back to the actual answer, but it’s hard to stay on it."
